At the same time, same-lemma main and light centroids are consistently closer than mismatched main--light lemma pairs, supporting continued lexical relatedness. In a seven-way prediction task restricted to light uses, verb identity remains recoverable after the target is masked, with UrduBERT achieving 0.866 accuracy and 0.852 macro-F1. UrduBERT also retains 0.782 accuracy under a preceding-form-disjoint evaluation, indicating generalization beyond repeated local verb combinations. These findings provide computational evidence consistent with Butt's account that Urdu light verbs differ systematically from their main uses while retaining lemma-specific and verb-specific representational structure.

</details>


### [101] [The Limits of Automatic Evaluation of Creativity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23705)
*Alessandro Tutone,Giorgio Franceschelli,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM生成文本的自动评估方法能否可靠捕捉人类对创造力的判断。作者收集了人类对WritingPrompts数据集中人类和AI生成短故事的11个创造力维度的人工评估，并与自动化客观指标和LLM-as-a-Judge评估进行比较。结果发现自动评估与人类判断存在显著错位：LLM评委系统性偏好AI生成的故事，而广泛使用的自动指标与人类判断几乎零相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的文本在创造性领域越来越接近甚至超越人类表现，但如何评估LLM生成内容的创造力仍是一个重大挑战。现有自动评估方法是否真正反映人类对创造力的判断是未知的，这促使研究者系统性地检验自动评估工具的有效性。

Method: 使用WritingPrompts数据集，收集人类对人类和AI生成短故事的11个维度创造力评估；然后比较这些人工判断与两类自动化评估方法（客观指标和LLM-as-a-Judge）的结果，通过相关性和偏好分析评估对齐程度。

Result: 实验发现自动评估与人类判断之间存在显著错位：LLM作为评委系统性地偏好AI生成的故事，偏爱其风格化特征而忽视人类文本的不可预测性等品质；广泛使用的自动指标与人类判断几乎零相关，无法捕捉创造力的重要维度。

Conclusion: 当前自动评估创造性文本的方法存在根本性局限，创造力作为多维度、主观的性质难以被简化为计算指标。该研究强调了需要更精细、更可靠的评估方法来衡量LLM的创造性能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of generating text that challenges human performance in domains requiring creativity, yet evaluating creativity in LLM-generated content remains a significant challenge. Here, we investigate whether current automatic evaluation methods can reliably capture human judgments of creativity. We collect human evaluations of human- and AI-generated short stories from the WritingPrompts dataset across 11 dimensions of creativity, and compare these judgments with automated objective metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Our experiments reveal substantial misalignment between automatic evaluations and human assessments. In particular, LLM-based judges exhibit a systematic preference for AI-generated stories, consistently favoring their stylistic characteristics over the unpredictability and other qualities of human-authored texts. Furthermore, correlation analyses show that widely used automatic metrics exhibit near-zero alignment with human judgments across both human- and AI-generated stories, suggesting that they fail to capture important dimensions of creativity. These findings highlight fundamental limitations in current approaches to the automatic evaluation of creative text and underscore the difficulty of reducing the multidimensional and subjective nature of creativity to computational metrics.

</details>


### [102] [ADE: Agentic Data Evolution Framework for Human-Centered Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23719)
*Yang Yu,Yilin Jiang,Zexuan Fei,Yiming Luo,Xingkai Song,Kaiyi Huang,Aimin Zhou,Xin Lin,Fei Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Data Evolution（ADE），一个以数据为中心的框架，通过循环的Observation-Variation-Selection（OVS）流程和稳态准入机制作为质量棘轮，在无可靠验证信号的情况下保守地门控数据快照更新，实现合成监督数据的持续跨轮改进。在DEV300上，内在胜率从50%提升至75.81%，外在胜率从55.20%提升至68.86%，盲审专家偏好度达66.11%，收益可扩展到不同后训练方法、模型规模和任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与人类中心目标对齐面临两大难题：一是目标不可执行且依赖上下文，导致验证困难、监督难以规模化；二是合成数据虽能扩展覆盖范围，但弱验证将瓶颈从生成转移到选择环节，噪声信号会破坏迭代精炼过程并引发静默性能回退。

Method: ADE将合成监督组织为演化的数据快照，通过闭环的Observation-Variation-Selection（OVS）流程改进快照质量。核心机制是稳态准入机制（steady-state admission mechanism），充当"质量棘轮"，以保守策略门控数据更新，确保跨轮稳定改进。

Result: 在DEV300上，ADE将内在胜率从50%提升至75.81%，外在胜率从55.20%提升至68.86%。盲审专家评估显示66.11%偏好ADE演化后的答案。性能提升在多种后训练方法、模型规模和超越目标弱可验证教育任务的场景中保持一致。

Conclusion: ADE通过"质量棘轮"式的保守数据演化机制，有效解决了弱验证条件下合成数据选择与迭代精炼的不稳定性问题，证明了数据演化策略在对齐不可执行目标时具有显著的通用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models to human-centered objectives is difficult when targets are non-executable and context-dependent, limiting reliable verification and scalable supervision. Although synthetic data expands coverage, weak verification shifts the bottleneck from generation to selection. Noisy signals destabilize iterative refinement and can cause silent regressions. We propose Agentic Data Evolution (ADE), a data-centric framework that organizes synthetic supervision as evolving data snapshots. ADE improves data snapshots through a closed-loop Observation-Variation-Selection (OVS) procedure, where a steady-state admission mechanism acts as a quality ratchet that conservatively gates updates for sustained cross-round improvement. We validate these improvements through complementary intrinsic trend tracking and extrinsic post-training evaluation. On DEV300, ADE raises the intrinsic win rate from 50% to 75.81% and the extrinsic win rate from 55.20% to 68.86%, consistent performance gains across diverse benchmarks. Blind expert evaluation further confirms this, with a 66.11% preference for evolved answers. These gains extend across post-training methods, model scales, and tasks beyond the target weakly verifiable educational objectives. Resources are available at https://github.com/ZeroLoss-Lab/Agentic-Data-Evolution.

</details>


### [103] [What Reaches Expert Review? Representation, Structural Screening, and Candidate-Form Dependence in AI-Assisted Item Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23766)
*Christopher Brooks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两项关联的计算机模拟研究（涉及32,000个Big Five人格条目），追踪了从语义表征、结构评估到候选构念构建的完整流程，发现全局统计层面的稳定性掩盖了局部内容层面（如条目措辞、构念证据归属）的重大不稳定性。计算评估器并非生成与专家评审之间的中性技术设施，而是可检查、可修改的测量设计组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于AI的条目生成与专家评审之间，存在一个计算评估环节，其决策通常被视为纯技术性的前置步骤。然而，表征方式、结构简化和筛选策略实际上决定了心理测量学家最终能看到的条目和证据。作者旨在揭示这些"技术细节"如何实质性地影响测量内容，挑战其被视为中性基础设施的默认假设。

Method: 采用两项相互关联的in-silico研究设计：从固定源群体出发，构建32,000个Big Five人格条目，沿语义表征→结构评估→候选构念构建的完整流水线进行追踪。系统性地改变嵌入配置与资格筛选策略，比较不同条件下语义几何的一致性、条目存活情况、构念证据归属以及最终提交给评审的表单内容。

Result: 虽然不同配置下语义几何大致一致，但局部差异具有实质性后果：相同措辞获得不同构念证据、不同条目存活、预期属性在社区对应性提升的同时消失。在最终评审边界处，两种资格政策都填满了所有内容单元，但呈现了不同措辞；跨嵌入配置的包容性主表单共享的中位共同条目仅为40项中的6项。全局摘要的稳定性掩盖了心理测量学家所接收内容的深层不稳定性。

Conclusion: 计算评估器不是AI生成与专家判断之间的中性基础设施，而是测量设计中可检查、可修订的一部分。全局统计指标的表面稳定不等同于测量内容的实质稳定性，研究者在部署计算评估流程时应将其视为测量设计决策的内在环节而非技术附件。

Abstract: Between AI-assisted item generation and expert review sits a computational evaluator whose decisions are usually treated as technical preliminaries. Yet representation, structural reduction, and selection policy determine which items and evidence psychometricians ever receive. Across two linked in-silico studies of 32,000 selected Big Five items, we followed fixed source populations from semantic representation through structural evaluation and candidate-form construction. Broad agreement in semantic geometry concealed consequential local differences: identical wording acquired different construct evidence, different items survived, and intended attributes could disappear even as community correspondence improved. These sensitivities also differed across generated source populations. At the final review boundary, both eligibility policies filled every content cell in every evaluable form, yet they presented different wording. Across embedding configurations, inclusive primary forms shared a median of only 6 of 40 items, reflecting the total downstream consequence of changing representation across structural evidence and ranking. The apparent stability of global summaries and complete forms therefore concealed instability in the content reaching psychometricians. The computational evaluator is not neutral infrastructure between generation and expertise; it is an inspectable and revisable part of measurement design.

</details>


### [104] [When Youth Enter The Chat: An Epistemic Shift in the Validation of LLM-Based Measures of Student Talk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23780)
*Liliana Santos-Deonizio,James Malamut,Ramón Martínez,Dorottya Demszky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前用LLM测量学生课堂话语的常见做法（基于转录文本、以成人专家标注为基准、用F1分数评估），指出这些方法对种族和语言边缘化青年不公平且缺乏意义。作者呼吁将青年纳入研究过程，让他们作为知识权威参与对话语的分析。通过一项针对8年级数学课堂中多语言青年的案例研究，作者发现学生对自己数学谈话经历的解读与LLM测量结果之间存在错位，学生挑战了LLM的分类和编码方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被广泛用于大规模测量学生话语（如话轮移动、协作、话语公平性），但现有验证方式（与成人专家标注对比、留出集评估、F1分数）不足以确保这些测量对教学和学习有意义且公平，尤其对种族和语言边缘化青年而言，因为仅基于转录文本的测量使学生的语言失去了情境化背景。

Method: 在8年级数学课堂中开展案例研究，选取四名焦点学生，采用多种民族志导向方法对学生的课堂对话进行再情境化，包括参与式观察、访谈、焦点小组和成员核查（member checks），将青年与研究者、LLM置于对话中，使青年成为知识权威。

Result: 研究发现学生对其自身数学谈话经历的解读与LLM基于转录文本的测量结果之间存在系统性错位。学生不仅质疑LLM的分类结果，还质疑用于测量其谈话的编码方案本身，这揭示了仅依赖LLM和成人专家视角的测量工具在理解边缘化青年话语方面存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 为了确保LLM测量学生话语的方法对教学和学习有意义且公平，必须将青年纳入知识生产的过程，让青年与研究者和LLM共同拥有认识论权威，以再情境化学生对话并为LLM测量提供关键补充和修正，而非仅仅依赖成人专家标注和量化指标。

Abstract: LLMs are being used increasingly to measure aspects of student discourse (e.g. talk moves, collaboration, equity of voice) at scale. Typically, LLM-based measures of student talk use transcriptions of classroom conversations that only include verbal contributions, which de-contextualize student language. Common practices for validating these measures include comparing outputs against expert annotations by adults, using held out evaluation sets and F1 scores. We argue that these approaches are insufficient to ensure that such measures are meaningful and equitable for teaching and learning, particularly for racially and linguistically marginalized youth. In order to center the youth whose talk is being analyzed, re-contextualizing these classroom conversations and engaging youth in the research process is necessary. Sharing epistemic authority with youth, ultimately, centers their point of view and adds crucial nuance to the analysis of their talk that adult experts, researchers, and LLMs cannot provide. In a case study of multilingual youth in one 8th-grade math classroom, we address the epistemic exclusion of youth by employing multiple ethnographically-oriented methods to re-contextualize student conversations and center youth as epistemic authorities in conversation with researchers and LLMs. We conducted participant observations, interviews, focus groups, and member checks with four focal students. Findings reveal that there were misalignments between students' interpretations of their own math talk experiences and the LLM-based measures of their talk. Students contested both the LLM classifications and the coding scheme used to measure their talk, highlighting the need for youth to be involved in the epistemic process of producing knowledge about their experiences.

</details>


### [105] [Inter-dimension Dependence for Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Open-Ended Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23783)
*Haoyuan Li,Snigdha Chaturvedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CorrGap指标来量化LLM-as-a-judge在多维度开放文本评估中的维度间依赖问题，并提出了DimCheck方法来通过逐步移除思维链中不相关证据来缓解该问题。实验表明DimCheck在三个LLM和四个任务上优于强基线，且小模型可以以更低推理成本近似大模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge被广泛用于开放文本生成的评估，但这类评估通常涉及多个维度。可靠的评估器应该独立地对每个目标维度进行打分，然而现有LLM评估器在评估某一维度时可能会受到其他非目标维度的影响（即维度间依赖），这一问题尚未被系统量化研究。

Method: 首先提出CorrGap指标，通过比较不同文本分组中LLM预测分数与真实分数之间相关性的差异来衡量维度间依赖程度。其次提出DimCheck方法，采用逐步迭代的方式从LLM生成的思维链（COT）中移除与目标维度无关的证据，从而缓解维度间依赖。

Result: 实验发现维度间依赖在各类LLM评估器中普遍存在；DimCheck能够有效缓解维度间依赖，并在三个LLM和四个任务上超越强基线；此外，较小的训练LLM可以在DimCheck中接近大模型的表现，同时推理成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 维度间依赖是LLM评估器中的普遍问题，CorrGap提供了量化该问题的有效工具，而DimCheck证明了通过逐步清理思维链证据可以在不牺牲性能的前提下缓解该问题，并具备低成本部署的潜力。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge methods are widely used for evaluating the quality of generated open-ended text. Such evaluations are generally multi-dimensional, since the error patterns in texts can be different for different dimensions. Therefore, reliable LLM judges should evaluate each target dimension independently. To quantify the extent to which LLM judges depend on non-target dimensions when evaluating a target dimension, i.e., inter-dimension dependence, we propose CorrGap. To measure this, CorrGap uses the difference in correlations between LLM-predicted scores and ground truth scores across different groups of texts. Using CorrGap, we show that inter-dimension dependence is pervasive across LLM judges in open-ended text evaluation tasks. To mitigate inter-dimension dependence, we propose DimCheck, a method that iteratively removes unrelated evidence from COTs generated by LLM judges in a step-wise way. We show that DimCheck mitigates inter-dimension dependence and outperforms strong baselines across three LLMs and four tasks. We also show that smaller trained LLMs can approximate larger LLMs in DimCheck, with much lower inference costs.

</details>


### [106] [Giga-Embeddings: Mixture-of-Experts Encoders for High-Throughput Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23806)
*Egor Kolodin,Egor Krasnoperov,Evgeniy Kosarev,Fyodor Minkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Giga-Embeddings，一个文本嵌入模型家族，旨在兼顾强检索质量与高效推理。其最大模型为稀疏10B参数的Mixture-of-Experts编码器（约1.8B活跃参数），在英语、俄语、多语言和代码MTEB基准上均取得家族内最优聚合性能，同时通过vLLM实现了114.5k tokens/sec的吞吐量，比稠密3B模型高出25%，比外部系统高出1.56-2.65倍。家族还包括稠密3B编码器及蒸馏得到的480M紧凑模型（在俄语MTEB上达70.98分，超越FRIDA且参数少42%）。三个模型权重均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本嵌入模型往往在检索质量与推理效率之间存在权衡：高质量模型通常参数庞大且推理开销高，难以实际部署；而紧凑模型又常常牺牲检索性能。Giga-Embeddings旨在同时实现强检索质量与高效服务能力，填补这一空白。

Method: 设计了三类模型：(1) 稀疏10B参数的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）编码器，每token仅激活约1.8B参数，既保留大模型容量又控制推理开销；(2) 稠密3B编码器，用于平衡性能与资源消耗；(3) 蒸馏得到的480M紧凑模型，采用维度无关的目标函数来对齐教师与学生模型之间的相似度分布，实现知识蒸馏。

Result: 稀疏10B模型在英语、俄语、多语言和代码四个MTEB基准上均取得家族内最佳聚合表现；在vLLM基准（1024-token输入）中达到114.5k tokens/sec吞吐量，较稠密3B模型提升25%，是外部系统的1.56-2.65倍；480M蒸馏模型在俄语MTEB上得分为70.98，超越FRIDA且参数减少42%。

Conclusion: Giga-Embeddings通过MoE稀疏架构与知识蒸馏策略，成功在检索质量与推理效率之间取得了良好平衡，验证了稀疏MoE编码器在嵌入任务中的有效性，并提供了从高效率到高性能的完整模型谱系，所有模型均已开源。

Abstract: We introduce Giga-Embeddings, a family of text embedding models designed to combine strong retrieval quality with efficient serving. Its largest member is a sparse 10B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts encoder with approximately 1.8B active parameters per token. Across English, Russian, multilingual, and code MTEB benchmarks, this model achieves the strongest aggregate performance within the family on all four evaluated suites. In our vLLM benchmark with 1024-token inputs, it processes 114.5k tokens per second, providing 25 percent higher throughput than the dense 3B model and 1.56-2.65x the throughput of the evaluated external systems. The family also includes a dense 3B encoder and a distilled 480M encoder for tighter compute and memory budgets. We train the compact model using a dimension-agnostic objective that aligns teacher and student similarity distributions. The resulting 480M model scores 70.98 on Russian MTEB, surpassing FRIDA while using 42 percent fewer parameters. We release all three model checkpoints.

</details>


### [107] [From Preferences to Principles: Rubric-Based Alignment for Grounded Knowledge Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23812)
*Aman Saini,Priyanshu Kumar,Eric Peng,Kai Yuan,Harsh Girase,Wanming Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于rubric（评分细则）的奖励框架，通过检索证据生成查询特定的多维rubric，为开放域问答提供细粒度的后训练监督信号，在三大评估轴上分别比指令微调基线和扁平rubric变体提升6.5%和4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域问答中设计有效的奖励信号极具挑战性，因为高质量回答需要同时满足多个质量维度，而单一的标量目标难以捕捉这些多维质量要求。

Method: 提出一种基于rubric的奖励框架：生成基于检索证据、查询特定的rubrics，并将其分解为多个质量维度（如内容、依据、指令遵循等），在后训练阶段提供细粒度监督信号。该方法结合了基于证据的条件化rubric生成与多维质量分解两个关键设计。

Result: 在构成、依据和指令遵循三个评估轴上，相比指令微调基线平均提升6.5%，相比扁平rubric变体平均提升4%，且在所有评估数据集上保持一致增益。基于检索证据的条件化rubric改善了事实支撑，多维分解的rubric则进一步提升了连贯性、组织性和对查询要求的遵循度。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于证据的多维rubrics能为复杂的开放域问答提供更有效的奖励监督信号。

Abstract: Designing effective reward signals for open-domain question answering is challenging because high-quality responses must simultaneously satisfy multiple aspects of answer quality that are difficult to capture with a holistic scalar objective. We introduce a rubric-based reward framework that generates query-specific rubrics grounded in retrieved evidence and decomposed into multiple quality dimensions, providing fine-grained supervision during post-training. Averaged across three evaluation axes (composition, grounding, and instruction-following), our approach improves over the instruction-tuned baseline by 6.5% and over flat rubric variants by 4%, with consistent gains across all evaluation datasets. Conditioning rubrics on retrieved evidence improves factual support, while decomposing rubrics into quality-specific dimensions further improves coherence, organization, and adherence to query requirements. Our results show that grounded, multi-dimensional rubrics provide more effective reward supervision for complex open-domain question answering.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Static and Linear: What Attention Constraints Best Fit Human Reading Times?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23818)
*Lanni Bu,Xiulin Yang,Christian Clark,Alex Warstadt,Ethan Gotlieb Wilcox*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种基于注意力的记忆约束机制对Transformer语言模型拟合人类阅读行为数据的影响，发现内容敏感的约束比距离约束更能预测人类阅读时间，且动态记忆课程下心理测量拟合与语法能力出现分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型被广泛用作人类语言处理的认知模型，但其注意力机制能够无损访问全部前文上下文，这与人类有限的记忆系统不符。已有研究大多孤立地探讨单一记忆约束，缺乏系统性比较，因此需要全面评估不同记忆约束机制对模型拟合人类行为数据的影响。

Method: 作者系统比较了多种基于注意力的记忆约束机制，包括距离敏感约束和内容敏感约束，在不同模型规模和训练语料下进行评估。同时对比了静态约束（训练中约束强度固定）与动态记忆课程（约束随训练变化），从心理测量预测力（人类阅读时间）和语法能力两个维度进行评价。

Result: 内容敏感的记忆约束在拟合人类阅读时间上表现最优，持续优于基于距离的约束。在动态记忆课程条件下，心理测量拟合与语法能力之间出现分离现象。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了记忆约束类型对模型认知仿真的关键影响，并指出动态记忆课程下心理测量拟合与语法能力存在解离，说明Transformer无法作为一概而论的认知模型。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widely used as models of human language processing, yet their attention mechanisms allow lossless access to the full preceding context, unlike the limited memory systems of humans. We hypothesize that installing memory constraints into transformers' attention mechanisms can improve their fit to human behavioral data. While previous work has explored individual constraints in isolation, we conduct a systematic comparison of multiple attention-based memory mechanisms across different model sizes and training corpora, evaluating both psychometric predictive power for human reading times and grammatical competence. We additionally compare static constraints, in which the constraint strength is fixed throughout training, to dynamic memory curricula. We find that constraints that are sensitive to the content of intervening tokens consistently achieve the highest alignment with human reading times, outperforming distance-based constraints. We observe a dissociation between psychometric fit and grammatical competence under dynamic memory curricula, suggesting that Transformers cannot serve as a one-size-fits-all cognitive model.

</details>


### [109] [Mitigating Exploration Bias in RL for Multi-Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23830)
*Mian Zhang,Yueqin Yin,Kaiyu He,Peilin Wu,Xinlu Zhang,Mingyuan Zhou,Zhiyu Zoey Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种指标来度量指令跟随中RL训练的探索偏差，并通过两阶段框架（行为引导+稀缺性感知奖励）缓解该偏差，显著提升模型在三个可验证指令跟随基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练配方在提升LLM指令跟随能力时存在探索偏差问题：当提示中包含多个指令时，优化偏向容易的指令。原因有二：1) 策略模型初始难以满足困难指令，无法触发成功的RL探索；2) 典型RL训练使用累积奖励（满足指令的数量），对所有指令一视同仁，导致模型倾向于完成容易指令以获取相同奖励。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) Behavioral Bootstrapping——在RL之前进行轻量级拒绝采样微调，以激活困难指令的探索；2) Scarcity-Aware Rewards——基于指令的经验稀缺性分配奖励的新RL奖励函数。同时提出两种指标来衡量指令跟随中的探索偏差。

Result: 所提出的指标与模型性能高度相关；所提出的方法释放了RL训练的潜力，最佳模型在三个可验证的指令跟随基准上显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 探索偏差是多指令RL训练中的一个关键瓶颈，通过行为引导激活困难指令和基于稀缺性的奖励设计可以有效缓解该偏差，提升模型遵循复杂指令的能力。

Abstract: RL has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing the instruction following capabilities of LLMs. While existing training recipes achieve substantial gains, we find that they suffer from exploration bias towards easy instructions when the training data has multiple instructions in a prompt. This bias is caused by two main reasons: 1) the policy model's initial ability to satisfy hard instructions is too low to trigger successful exploration during RL training, so the optimization is biased towards easy instructions; and 2) canonical RL training recipes typically employ a cumulative reward (the number of instructions fulfilled), treating all instructions equally, which biases the policy model towards fulfilling easy instructions to obtain the same amount of reward. To address these issues, we first propose two metrics to measure the exploration bias in instruction following and then introduce a two-stage framework to alleviate it: 1) Behavioral Bootstrapping, a lightweight rejection sampling fine-tuning stage before RL to activate hard instructions; and 2) Scarcity-Aware Rewards, a new RL reward function that assigns rewards to instructions based on their empirical scarcity. Experiments show that the proposed metrics are highly correlated with model performance, and our methods unleash the potential of RL training: our best models outperform the baselines by a significant margin across three verifiable instruction following benchmarks. We release codes at https://github.com/mianzhang/MulIF.

</details>


### [110] [Does Episodic Memory Help Close the Lexical Frequency Gap in Sensitivity to Syntactic Contrasts? A Test Using Retrieval-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23851)
*Jing Liu,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于互补学习系统（CLS）理论，提出海马体情景记忆机制可使语法判断对词汇频率具鲁棒性。通过k近邻检索增强语言模型（kNN-LM）作为情景记忆的实例化，发现检索增强缩小了高、低频词项在句法对比测试中的性能差距，且结构信息对有效检索至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为语法知识及其经验测试对词汇频率不敏感，但神经网络语言模型表现出对词频的高度敏感性。作者希望利用CLS理论解释频率鲁棒性如何产生，并验证海马体式情景记忆（快速编码和检索具体经验）能否弥补参数模型中弱化了的低频表征，从而缩小频率差距。

Method: 以k最近邻语言模型（kNN-LM）作为情景记忆机制的实例化，将显式实例存储与参数化模型结合。使用频率分层测试项的句法对比测试，比较检索增强前后模型在高频与低频词项上的性能差距，并分析了不同句法现象、不同预训练数据规模下的效果，同时检验结构信息与语义相似性对检索的影响。

Result: 检索增强显著缩小的频率差距，且此效应在不同句法现象和不同预训练规模（儿童数据和大规模数据）下一致；结构信息对有效检索至关重要，而语义相似性帮助甚微；但频率差距仅被缩小而非完全消除。

Conclusion: 结果支持情景记忆补偿弱参数表征的假设，暗示海马体式记忆机制有助于语言模型的频率鲁棒性。作者提出优先重加权检索实例、改进结构信息的表征与检索策略、灵活配置存储与检索方式等未来方向以进一步优化情景记忆在语言模型中的实现。

Abstract: Grammatical knowledge and how it is empirically tested are typically considered robust to the frequency of the lexical items in the expressions. However, neural network-based models of grammaticality exhibit high sensitivity to lexical frequency. We draw upon Complementary Learning Systems theory to test the hypothesis that robustness to lexical frequency can arise via a hippocampal episodic memory mechanism, which enables rapid encoding and retrieval of specific experiences and allows learners to leverage them when processing rare patterns. We use retrieval-augmented language models as an instantiation of such an episodic memory mechanism (specifically, $k$-nearest-neighbor language models that augment parametric models with explicit instance storage), and test whether this augmentation helps close the lexical frequency gap that vanilla language models exhibit in syntactic contrast tests. Using syntactic contrasts with frequency-stratified test items, we find that retrieval augmentation narrows the performance gap between high- and low-frequency items, consistent with episodic memory compensating for weak parametric representations. This benefit is consistent across different syntactic phenomena and across models pretrained on child-realistic and large-scale data. Additionally, we show that structural information is critical for effective retrieval, whereas semantic similarity alone provides little benefit. While these are promising proof-of-concept results supporting our hypothesis, the frequency gap is narrowed rather than fully closed. Based on our analyses, we propose preferential reweighting of retrieved instances, better representations and retrieval strategies for structural information, and flexible configurations of storage and retrieval as promising future directions for improving the implementation of episodic memory in language models.

</details>


### [111] [Names Can Hurt: Spotting Slopsquatting Risks Caused by Package Name Hallucinations in Local Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23897)
*Akash Raj,Sargam Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双层检测器来应对"slopsquatting"攻击——即代码生成大模型幻觉编造PyPI包名，攻击者预先注册该包名从而将幻觉转化为供应链攻击。第一层为确定性PyPI存在性检查，第二层为基于包名及PyPI元数据十项特征的随机森林分类器，通过导入名称协调器衔接两层，并嵌入LangGraph状态机在升级温度下重试，失败后路由到更强的备用模型。在300条精选提示上，76%的运行生成无幻觉代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成语言模型在编造不存在的Python包名时，攻击者可通过预先在PyPI上注册该名称，将模型幻觉转化为实际的供应链攻击（即slopsquatting）。现有防御手段不足，亟需一种能够检测并阻断此类幻觉包名的系统。

Method: 提出双层检测器：第一层为确定性PyPI存在性检查；第二层为基于十个特征（来自包名及其PyPI元数据）的随机森林分类器。引入"导入名称协调器"解决'import cv2'与'pip install opencv-python'这类名称不对应问题而不构成安全绕过。检测器嵌入LangGraph状态机，在逐步升高温度下重试，多次失败后路由至更强的备用模型。

Result: 在300条精选提示下，76%的运行产生无幻觉代码。主模型在28.7%的运行中耗尽重试预算；模型内重试恢复约四分之一的失败案例，跨模型回退进一步恢复剩余案例中的16.5%。用户研究（n=24）平均满意度4.4/5，21/24表示采用意向。四项发现：约半数被标记的幻觉其实已是PyPI上已注册的低质量仿冒包；幻觉率随提示对抗性几乎线性增长（从日常编码的0-10%到slopsquat诱饵的40-73%）；较弱的模型对10个直接诱饵中6个未经辅助即拒绝，显示指令微调提供基线防御；主模型与备用模型同属一家时，约84%的主模型失败会在备用模型上重演，支持跨模型族配对。

Conclusion: 该双层检测器能有效缓解slopsquatting攻击，但研究发现幻觉包中约一半是PyPI上已存在的低质量仿冒品——这类攻击更隐蔽且依赖分类器而非确定性检查捕获。同时建议采用跨模型族的回退配对以提高防御鲁棒性，识别指令微调作为基线防御的重要价值。

Abstract: When a code generating language model fabricates a Python package name, an adversary who has pre-registered that name on PyPI can convert that hallucination into a supply chain compromise. This event has been termed as 'slopsquatting'. We propose a two layer detector to counter this issue. The first layer performs a deterministic PyPI existence check. The second is a Random Forest classifier trained on ten features derived from the package name and its PyPI metadata. An import name reconciler bridges the two, resolving cases such as 'import cv2' versus 'pip install opencv-python' without a security bypass. The detector is embedded in a LangGraph state machine that retries at escalating temperatures and, on repeated failure, routes to a stronger fallback model. Across 300 curated prompts, the pipeline produces hallucination free code on 76% of runs. The primary exhausts its retry budget on 28.7%; intra model retries recover roughly a quarter of those, and cross model fallback recovers a further 16.5% of the remainder. Four findings have been observed. First, half of the flagged hallucinations are packages already registered on PyPI, as low quality lookalikes of well known projects, caught by the classifier rather than the deterministic layer (e.g., pil, faiss, tabula, haystack). Second, hallucination rate scales almost linearly with prompt adversariality, from 0 to 10% on routine coding to 40 to 73% on slopsquat baits. Third, the weaker primary refused 6 of 10 direct baits unaided, suggesting recent instruction tuning provides a baseline defense. Fourth, when primary and fallback share a model family, approximately 84% of primary failures recur on the fallback, motivating cross family pairing. A user study (n = 24) reports mean satisfaction 4.4 out of 5 and 21 of 24 stated adoption intent.

</details>


### [112] [Investigating Knowledge Transfer Across Interactive Dialogue Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23969)
*Filippo Momentè,Mir Nafis Sharear Shopnil,Andrea de Varda,Pavel Merinov,Raffaella Bernardi,Oswald Lanz,Alessandro Suglia,Alessandro Torcinovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了不同对话游戏之间的知识迁移能力，通过在clembench套件上微调LLM模型，利用二元整数优化程序构建任务迁移图以及任务向量两种分析方法，发现部分游戏从迁移中获得比微调更大的收益，其中视觉空间类游戏迁移效果最佳；同时发现基于相似度的任务向量方法虽能捕捉游戏角色关系但几乎无法捕捉迁移模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话游戏需要复杂的认知技能，语言既是理解游戏规则的接口也是执行动作的接口，因此假设在特定语言游戏上的训练可能增强对其他任务也相关的能力。研究者希望探究不同对话游戏之间的知识迁移机制。

Method: 在clembench套件的游戏上微调LLM模型，进行两种分析：1) 使用Zamir等人(2018)的二元整数优化程序，以任务性能为主要指标推导任务迁移性图；2) 计算Ilharco等人(2022)的任务向量以研究不同微调模型之间的相似性及其任务迁移能力。

Result: 任务迁移图分析发现：部分游戏从迁移中获得的收益比直接微调更多，且视觉空间家族的游戏（如探索类游戏）迁移效果最佳。任务向量分析发现：基于相似度的方法能够捕捉游戏-角色之间的关系，但几乎无法捕捉迁移性模式，表明需要更复杂的指标。

Conclusion: 对话游戏之间存在有意义的任务迁移知识，尤其视觉空间类游戏具有最佳迁移性；但简单的相似度方法不足以预测迁移效果，未来需要使用更复杂的度量标准来深入研究任务迁移模式。

Abstract: Dialogue games represent a challenging setting where complex cognitive skills are required to accomplish tasks while coordinating with other players. Considering that language represents an interface for both understanding the game rules and executing actions, it is reasonable to assume that training on a specific language game will enhance specific capabilities that might be relevant for other tasks as well. Motivated by this rationale, in this paper, we investigate how knowledge transfers across different dialogue games. We study transferability by finetuning LLM models on games from the clembench suite (Chalamalasetti et al., 2023) and performing two analyses: i) we derive a task-transferability graph using a binary integer optimization program from Zamir et al. (2018), using task performance as the main metric; and ii) we compute task vectors (Ilharco et al., 2022) for each game to study similarities across finetuned models and their task transferability. In our first analysis, we find that some games benefit more from transfer than finetuning, and that the visuospatial family (e.g., exploration games) transfers best. With our task vector analysis instead, we find that similarity-based approaches capture game-role relationships but almost no transferability patterns, suggesting that more complex metrics are required.

</details>


### [113] [AgentSpec: Speculative Decoding for Batch Inference of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24004)
*Xin Wang,Ziming Miao,Yi Zhu,Hui Shen,Zhongwei Wan,Fan Yang,Mi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentSpec，一种针对LLM Agent应用的投机解码算法，通过结构隔离草稿生成和冗余感知预算分配解决大规模批处理下投机解码速度退化的问题，在五个工作负载和四个模型家族上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Agent应用通常响应时间较长，投机解码虽能提升推理效率，但在大batch size下存在显著的速度退化问题，限制了其在真实场景中的部署。现有投机解码算法未能充分解决这一瓶颈。

Method: AgentSpec包含两个核心组件：1) 结构隔离草稿生成（structure-isolated drafting），将投机限制在Agent工作流的语义连贯片段内，减少无关语义路径的草稿生成，从而大幅降低拒绝率；2) 冗余感知预算分配（redundancy-aware budget allocation），利用Agent级信息更好地利用推理过程中动态释放的token预算。在vLLM上实现并评估。

Result: 在五个不同工作负载和四个不同模型家族（共四个模型）上进行了评估，在vLLM框架下实现，结果显示AgentSpec显著优于现有最先进的投机解码方法。

Conclusion: AgentSpec通过针对Agent工作流语义结构的优化，有效解决了大batch size下投机解码的速度退化问题，证明了利用Agent级信息进行投机解码优化是一种有效且实用的方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agent applications often incur high response time. Speculative decoding is a promising solution to improve the inference efficiency of LLM agents without impacting generation quality. However, state-of-the-art speculative decoding algorithms exhibit substantial speed degradation under large batch sizes, limiting their effectiveness to deploy in real-world agent applications. In this work, we first present a systematic analysis of speculative decoding for LLM agents and identify two dominant factors of speedup degradation: high rejection rate of speculative tokens, and under-utilization of dynamic token budgets.B ased on these observations, we propose AgentSpec, a speculative decoding algorithm that addresses the limitations of existing methods for LLM agents. AgentSpec incorporates structure-isolated drafting that constrains speculation to semantically coherent segments of the agent workflow, reducing the drafts of irrelevant semantic paths and achieving an extremely low rejection rate. Moreover, AgentSpec adopts redundancy-aware budget allocation that exploits agent-level information to better utilize the dynamically-free token budget during the agent inference. We implement and evaluate AgentSpec on five different workloads and four different models from four different LLM families in vLLM. Our results demonstrate the superiority of AgentSpec over state-of-the-arts.

</details>


### [114] [SAGE: From Direct Answering to Evidence-Grounded Inference for Chinese Ancient Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24011)
*Yuchuan Wu,Xuan Luo,Yinglian Zhu,Meng Fang,Xiangyang Xue,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE，一个基于证据的多智能体框架，将中国古文献理解重新定义为证据推理而非直接答案生成，通过任务感知规划、工具介导的证据获取、声明级验证和有界重规划等机制提升推理可靠性，在AncientDoc基准上超越直接回答基线并优于更大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在处理中国古文献理解时依赖不透明的单次生成范式，往往产生过度自信且缺乏充分依据的响应。古文献理解涉及复杂的视觉、语言和历史推理，需要更强的证据支撑和可验证性。

Method: SAGE采用多智能体框架，在受限共享状态运行时下协调专业智能体，包括：任务感知规划（task-aware planning）、工具介导的证据获取（tool-mediated evidence acquisition）、声明级验证（claim-level verification）和有界重规划（bounded replanning）。该设计支持有界证据搜索、答案修订以及证据不足时的弃权机制。

Result: 在AncientDoc基准上，SAGE在三个LVLM骨干网络上均一致优于直接回答基线。值得注意的是，仅使用Qwen3.5-9B的SAGE在大多数评估指标上超越了更大规模的单体LVLM，表明结构化的证据推理比单纯扩大模型规模更为重要。

Conclusion: SAGE验证了基于证据的结构化推理范式在古文献理解任务中的有效性，强调在专业领域中，多智能体协作与证据验证机制比扩大模型规模更具性价比和可靠性。

Abstract: Chinese ancient document understanding demands complex visual, linguistic, and historical reasoning. Current Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) typically rely on an opaque, single-pass generation paradigm, often producing overconfident and weakly grounded responses. To address this, we propose SAGE, an evidence-grounded multi-agent framework that reformulates Chinese ancient document understanding as evidence-grounded inference rather than direct answer generation. SAGE coordinates specialized agents for task-aware planning, tool-mediated evidence acquisition, claim-level verification, and bounded replanning under a constrained shared-state runtime. This design supports bounded evidence seeking, answer revision, and abstention when grounding is insufficient. Experiments on the AncientDoc benchmark show that SAGE consistently outperforms matched direct-answering baselines across three LVLM backbones. Remarkably, SAGE with Qwen3.5-9B surpasses much larger monolithic LVLMs on most evaluated metrics, highlighting the importance of structured, evidence-grounded inference beyond model scaling.

</details>


### [115] [Curved Inference II: Sleeper Agent Geometry - Extending Interpretability Beyond Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24037)
*Rob Manson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Anthropic的Sleeper Agents研究，提出一种无需人工触发词或监督式后门注入的自然主义方法，通过多轮上下文窗口模拟真实欺骗性推理。基于Curved Inference框架，引入语义表面积（A'）这一新指标，在不使用标签、探针或后门的情况下，通过几何结构（曲率、显著性、语义表面积）可靠预测语义分类，且测量精度能揭示被分类噪声隐藏的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于线性探针的后门检测方法依赖于线性可分性，但这可能只是人工后门注入的伪影而非自然涌现的欺骗性对齐的属性。自然训练中产生的复杂欺骗行为不太可能产生如此便利的线性信号，因此需要一种不依赖人工触发模式和后门注入的、能检测自然产生的欺骗性推理的方法。

Method: 1) 采用多轮上下文窗口的自然主义方法模拟真实欺骗推理，避免人工触发词和监督式后门插入；2) 基于Curved Inference框架分析曲率和显著性；3) 引入新的度量"语义表面积（A'）"，在未归一化的残差空间中量化意义构建的幅度和方向变化；4) 通过LLM共识对模型输出进行分类；5) 在五种提示策略和两个模型家族上验证几何结构与语义分类的关系。

Result: 几何结构可靠地预测语义分类，五种提示策略和两个模型家族在语义表面积上均存在统计学显著差异。测量精度能揭示被分类噪声隐藏的几何特征——某些策略从非显著（p=0.555）提升到显著（p=0.048），表明即使检测看似失败，复杂的推理仍会留下内在的几何模式。

Conclusion: 推理本身的形状编码了语义模式，无论模型是否学会了抑制欺骗的线性指标。这为在线性方法失效时提供了一条可扩展的、无监督的欺骗检测路径，表明几何分析可以作为探针方法的有效补充或替代方案。

Abstract: This paper extends Anthropic's Sleeper Agents research [1], which showed artificial backdoors persist through safety training & can be detected by linear probes with >99% accuracy [2]. However, probe-based detection relies on linear separability that may be an artefact of backdoor insertion rather than a property of naturally occurring deceptive alignment. Sophisticated deceptive behaviours emerging through natural training are unlikely to produce such convenient linear signals.
  We introduce a naturalistic methodology using multi-turn context windows that simulates realistic deceptive reasoning without artificial triggers or supervised backdoor insertion. Rather than binary trigger-response patterns, we examine how semantic complexity emerges through gradual context development.
  Building on our Curved Inference framework, we analyse curvature, salience, & introduce semantic surface area (A'), a new metric of representational work capturing both the magnitude & directional change of meaning construction in unnormalised residual space. Without backdoors, labels, or probes, we apply this framework to naturalistic deceptive prompts & classify model outputs via LLM consensus.
  Geometric structure reliably predicts semantic classification, with statistically significant differences in surface area across five prompt strategies & two model families. Critically, measurement precision can reveal geometric signatures hidden by classification noise - some strategies improve from non-significant (p = 0.555) to significant (p = 0.048). This validates that sophisticated reasoning creates intrinsic geometric patterns that persist even when detection appears to fail, suggesting the shape of inference itself encodes semantic patterns regardless of whether models have learned to suppress linear indicators of deception - a scalable, unsupervised path for detection when linear methods fail.

</details>


### [116] [When Less Is More: An Empirical Study of Minimal Responses in Counseling Dialogues and the Behavior of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24080)
*Zhiyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对心理咨询对话中的"最小回应"（如简短的共情语句和附和信号）进行了跨语言系统分析，提出基于长度和内容的两阶段过滤方法并结合大语言模型进行上下文验证，发现最小回应在人工数据集中常见但在LLM生成数据中严重不足，且现有LLM虽能在明确指令下生成最小回应，但难以自主判断其适用时机，基于合成数据训练的心理咨询专用模型表现更差，LLM评估器也会低估其交互价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理咨询对话系统与评估框架偏向于显式、信息丰富的回复，忽视了简短咨询师话语（如最小回应）在传达倾听、表达共情和鼓励来访者继续表达方面的交互价值，导致对话系统与评估机制对这一重要交互行为缺乏系统性的关注和衡量。

Method: 该方法包括：(1) 基于话语长度和内容的two-stage过滤方法，从多个跨语言心理咨询对话数据集中识别最小回应；(2) 利用大语言模型（LLM）进行上下文验证；(3) 在人工整理的咨询情境中评估当前LLM生成最小回应的能力及其自主判断时机的能力；(4) 评估LLM作为对话质量评判者对最小回应的评分倾向性。

Result: 研究发现：最小回应在人工收集的数据集中较为常见，但在LLM生成的数据中显著欠代表；强商业LLM在明确指令下能生成最小回应，但仍难以自主判断何时回应是恰当的；基于合成数据训练的心理咨询专用模型表现尤其差，倾向于生成更长、信息更丰富的回应；LLM评估器在最小回应交互上恰当时也可能低估其质量。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了心理咨询对话系统中对最小回应价值认识不足的问题，指出当前LLM对话生成和评估体系均未充分捕捉最小回应在心理咨询中的交互功能，为改进对话系统设计及训练数据策略提供了方向性启示。

Abstract: In psychological counseling, effective support is not always delivered through long, information-rich responses. Minimal responses, such as backchannel cues and concise empathic statements, help convey attentive listening, express empathy, and encourage clients to continue expressing themselves. However, existing counseling dialogue systems and evaluation frameworks often favor explicit, content-rich replies, overlooking the interactional value of brief counselor utterances. This paper presents a systematic cross-lingual analysis of minimal responses across multiple counseling dialogue datasets. We develop a two-stage filtering method based on utterance length and content, followed by contextual verification using a large language model (LLM). Our analysis shows that minimal responses are common in human-collected datasets but substantially underrepresented in LLM-generated ones. We further evaluate current LLMs in manually curated dialogue contexts where human counselors used minimal responses. The results show that strong commercial LLMs are capable of generating minimal responses when explicitly instructed, but still struggle to determine when such responses are appropriate. Counseling-specific models trained on synthetic data perform particularly poorly, tending instead to produce longer and more information-rich responses. Moreover, LLM-based response-quality evaluation may undervalue minimal responses, even when they are interactionally appropriate.

</details>


### [117] [PARTAB: Partition-Aware Reasoning with Structured Evidence for Scalable Table Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24082)
*Md Mahadi Hasan Nahid,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PARTAB（分区感知的表推理框架），通过构建结构化的证据接口，将查询相关的表证据表示为语义连贯、按行链接的表区域，并在列组和行级分区上进行层次化选择后组合证据进行答案生成，从而缓解LLM在大规模复杂表推理中的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在表推理中表现出色，但随着表格规模增大和复杂度提升，由于无关上下文的干扰以及证据定位困难，模型性能会显著下降。现有方法通常对全表或单一缩减视图进行推理，仍可能掩盖重要的行列关系，因此需要更结构化的证据构建方式来提升推理的可扩展性。

Method: PARTAB框架包含两个关键阶段：(1) 构建结构化的证据接口，将查询相关的证据表示为语义连贯、按行链接的表区域；(2) 在列组和行级分区上进行层次化选择，然后组合所选证据用于答案生成。

Result: PARTAB在多个表推理基准上（涵盖问答、事实验证和数值推理）一致地优于全表提示方法及多种近期表推理方法，在WikiTableQuestions和TabFact上表现突出，在数值推理任务上保持竞争力。额外分析表明，语义分区和目标化证据选择改善了证据定位，大幅减少了推理上下文，并在复杂表格上带来更大收益。

Conclusion: 结构化、分区感知的证据构建对于可扩展的表推理具有重要价值，PARTAB通过精细的证据组织和层次化选择有效解决了大表场景下LLM推理性能退化的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in table reasoning, but their effectiveness degrades as tables grow in size and complexity due to irrelevant context and difficulty localizing the evidence required for reasoning. Existing approaches typically reason over either the full table or a single reduced view, which can still obscure important row-column relationships. We introducePARTAB (Partition-Aware Reasoning overTables), a framework that constructs a structured evidence interface between the LLM and the table. PARTAB represents query-relevant evidence as semantically coherent, row-linked table regions and performs hierarchical selection over column groups and row-level partitions before composing the selected evidence for answer generation. We evaluate PARTAB on multiple table reasoning benchmarks, covering question answering, fact verification, and numerical reasoning. PARTAB consistently improves over full-table prompting and several recent table reasoning methods, achieving strong performance on WikiTableQuestions and TabFact while remaining competitive on numerical reasoning. Additional analyses show that semantic partitioning and targeted evidence selection improve evidence localization, substantially reduce the reasoning context, and provide larger benefits on complex tables. These results demonstrate the value of structured, partition aware evidence construction for scalable table reasoning.

</details>


### [118] [MC-CXR: A Multi-Context Chest X-ray Benchmark for Context-Induced Disruption in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24118)
*Junhyeok Lee,Songsoo Kim,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-CXR基准，用于评估视觉语言模型(VLM)在胸部X光片解读中，当上下文(文字报告或先前的影像)与图像证据冲突时，模型是否会错误地改变其原本基于图像的正确判断。通过对240个病例的2,522个实例评估10个VLM，发现误导性文本导致45.6-78.1%的切换错误率，而误导性视觉上下文仅导致35.7-61.7%，且文本误导下的错误预测74.6%与误导标签对齐，而视觉仅为17.6%，揭示了明显的文本-视觉不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM基准仅评估模型在孤立情境下能否正确回答问题，而未评估当合理的上下文(如检索报告、初步注释、既往影像)与图像证据冲突时，模型是否能够保持正确的图像驱动决策。在临床管线中，VLM通常在有上下文的场景中部署，因此上下文引发的错误判断是安全隐患，需要专门的基准来量化这种上下文诱导的决策破坏。

Method: 构建MC-CXR基准：从240个病例扩展为2,522个实例，采用配对扰动设计——固定当前图像和目标发现，同时提供匹配的可靠与误导性上下文(文本和既往CXR，有可视覆盖则添加)。定义三个任务族和两个配对指标：切换到错误率(switch-to-wrong rate)和上下文对齐错误率(context-aligned error rate)。评估10个VLM，涵盖开源通用、医学领域和闭源系统。

Result: 图像单独准确率是必要但不充分的条件。误导性文本源的切换率均值为45.6-78.1%，误导性视觉源为35.7-61.7%。在切换的预测中，文本上下文的74.6%与误导标签对齐，视觉上下文仅17.6%对齐，两者差距57.0个百分点(95% CI 50.9-62.8)。文本-视觉不对称性在标准化直接回答协议下观察到。

Conclusion: VLM在胸部X光解读中对误导性文本上下文高度敏感，即使图像单独预测准确，引入冲突文本也会导致大量决策翻转，且翻转倾向于跟随误导标签；相比之下，误导性视觉上下文的破坏力较小。这表明在临床部署中，文本上下文的可靠性审查比视觉上下文更为关键。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in clinical pipelines where a chest X-ray is interpreted alongside retrieved reports, preliminary notes, or prior imaging. Existing benchmarks measure whether models answer correctly in isolation, but not whether they preserve a correct image-only decision when plausible context conflicts with the image. We introduce Multi-Context Chest X-ray (MC-CXR), a benchmark of 240 cases expanded into 2,522 instances that isolates context-induced disruption through paired perturbation. Each case fixes the current image and target finding while presenting matched reliable and misleading context across text and prior CXR, with visual overlays where available. MC-CXR defines three task families and two paired metrics, the switch-to-wrong rate and the context-aligned error rate. We evaluate ten VLMs spanning open-source general, medical-domain, and closed-source systems. Image-only accuracy is necessary but insufficient. Mean switch rates range from 45.6-78.1% across misleading textual sources and 35.7-61.7% across misleading visual sources. Among switched predictions, 74.6% align with the misleading label for text versus 17.6% for visual context, a 57.0-point gap (95% CI 50.9-62.8). This text-visual asymmetry is observed under the standardized direct-answer protocol. The dataset is available on PhysioNet.

</details>


### [119] [TrustDABench: Benchmarking Reliability and Robustness of LLMs for Structured Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24145)
*Boshen Shi,Yize Liu,Chen Zhao,Ce Chi,Zhendong Wang,Xing Wang,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustDABench基准测试，从证据路径视角评估LLM在结构化数据分析中的可靠性与鲁棒性，包含2,340个经人工验证的扰动实例，评估8个代表性LLM后发现最佳可靠性仅24.21%平均MRS、最佳鲁棒性仅9.10%平均ASR，揭示模型在冲突证据识别和表示不变推理方面存在系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于分析电子表格、CSV等结构化数据，但产生看似正确的答案并不等于产生可信的分析。可信结果应受从用户问题到相关数据证据的有效路径支持，因此需要考察两个诊断性问题：当路径不存在时LLM能否拒绝回答或请求澄清（可靠性），以及当相同证据以不同表格形式表达时能否保持正确分析（鲁棒性）。

Method: 从证据路径视角出发，推导出19种扰动算子，并通过基于Agentic-LLM的生成框架进行实例化。构建了包含2,340个人工验证扰动实例的TrustDABench基准，并评估了8个代表性LLM。可靠性通过平均MRS（Matched Refusal/Specification）衡量，鲁棒性通过平均ASR（Attack Success Rate）衡量。

Result: 最佳可靠性结果仅为24.21%平均MRS（由GPT-5.5取得），最佳鲁棒性结果仍有9.10%平均ASR（由Claude-Sonnet-5取得）。模型很少检测到冲突证据，经常沿可执行但缺乏证据支持的分析路径前进，并对改变观察边界或跨表关系的扰动保持敏感。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在结构化数据分析的可靠性和鲁棒性上仍有巨大提升空间。更强的证据边界识别能力和表示不变推理能力是未来实现可靠结构化数据分析的关键方向。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to analyze spreadsheets, CSV files, and other structured data, but producing a correct-looking answer is not the same as producing a trustworthy analysis. A trustworthy result should be supported by a valid path from the user question to the relevant data evidence. This requirement creates two diagnostic questions: whether an LLM can refuse to answer or ask for clarification when such a path does not exist, and whether it can preserve the correct analysis when the same evidence is expressed in different table forms. We introduce TrustDABench, a benchmark that operationalizes these questions as reliability and robustness. Starting from the evidence-path view, we derive 19 perturbation operators and instantiate them through an Agentic-LLM-based generation framework. TrustDABench contains 2,340 human-verified perturbed instances, and we evaluate eight representative LLMs. The results show substantial headroom: the best reliability result is only 24.21% average MRS, achieved by GPT-5.5, while the best robustness result still has 9.10% average ASR, achieved by Claude-Sonnet-5. The failures are systematic: models rarely detect conflicting evidence, often continue along executable but unsupported analysis paths, and remain sensitive to perturbations that change observation boundaries or cross-table relations. These findings suggest that stronger evidence-boundary recognition and representation-invariant reasoning are still needed for reliable structured-data analysis.

</details>


### [120] [FireRedAudio: A General-Purpose Audio Language Model with Decoupled Continuous Representations for Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24168)
*Junjie Li,Xuelong Geng,Kun Xie,Feiyu Shen,Yichen Wu,Ziqi Dai,Yichen Han,Yan Jia,Kai Huang,Junjie Chen,Yixuan Li,Manzhen Wei,Fenglong Xie,Lei Xie,Xu Tang,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FireRedAudio是一个统一的音频语言模型，基于共享的9B参数LLM，在单一可训练自回归LLM内提供分离的连续输入表示——分别用于音频理解（Audio Encoder）和语音生成（RedAE路径）。该模型支持ASR、长达一小时的音频理解、零样本TTS、指令式TTS以及语义与声学语音编辑，在多项评估中达到竞争性或领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一音频模型需要同时处理理解与生成任务，但这两类任务对特征表示的需求存在根本矛盾：理解需要紧凑、适合长上下文建模的特征，而语音生成需要可重构、保留细粒度声学细节的特征。现有方法难以在此矛盾下实现单一模型中的统一音频理解与生成。

Method: 采用共享9B参数的LLM作为核心，为理解任务和生成任务分别设计独立的连续输入表示通路：理解输入通过专用的Audio Encoder处理；生成输入通过基于RedAE的通路处理。LLM直接生成文本，或通过条件化flow-matching DiT生成连续声学隐变量，并采用渐进式多任务训练策略。

Result: FireRedAudio在音频理解和多语言ASR上达到竞争性或领先性能；在零样本TTS中保持强内容准确性和说话人保留；在指令式TTS中指令遵循能力领先；在语义和声学语音编辑上相较于Ming-UniAudio-Edit有显著提升。长音频结构组织达到秒级时间戳精度。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了在中等等级规模的模型中，采用解耦的连续输入表示来统一音频理解与连续隐变量语音生成的可行性，为后续统一音频模型的设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: A unified audio model must recognize and understand linguistic, paralinguistic, and environmental information while supporting speech synthesis and editing. A key challenge is representation: understanding favors compact features suited to long-context modeling, whereas speech generation requires reconstructible features that preserve fine-grained acoustic detail. We introduce FireRedAudio, a general-purpose audio language model with a shared 9B-parameter LLM. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first publicly disclosed unified audio-language model to provide separate continuous input representations for understanding and generation within a single trainable autoregressive LLM. Audio to be recognized or analyzed is processed by a dedicated Audio Encoder, while speech inputs for generation use a RedAE-based pathway. The LLM directly generates text or conditions a flow-matching DiT to produce continuous acoustic latents. Through progressive multitask training, FireRedAudio supports ASR and audio understanding, with the latter extending to recordings of up to one hour, as well as zero-shot TTS, Instruct TTS, and semantic and acoustic speech editing. Its structured organization of long-form audio achieves second-level timestamp accuracy. Across comprehensive evaluations, FireRedAudio achieves competitive or leading performance in audio understanding and multilingual ASR, strong content accuracy and speaker preservation in zero-shot TTS, leading instruction following in Instruct TTS, and substantial improvements over Ming-UniAudio-Edit in both semantic and acoustic speech editing. These results demonstrate the viability of decoupled continuous input representations for unifying audio understanding and continuous-latent speech generation in a model of moderate scale. Our code is available at https://github.com/FireRedTeam/FireRedAudio.

</details>


### [121] [MemUse: Moving Memory Evaluation from Direct QA to Natural Integration in Long-Term Human-AI Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24189)
*Ryuichi Sumida,Koji Inoue,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现对话LLM的记忆系统在直接QA基准上的高分并不能预测真实用户满意度，因为基准衡量的是"被询问时的提取"，而真实对话需要"自然的语境整合"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评估基准（Direct QA）仅测试模型能否在直接提问时回忆事实，但缺乏证据表明这种能力与真实用户满意度相关。作者通过4个月的部署实验验证Direct QA与满意度的关联性。

Method: 在4个月的部署中（40名用户、1,872次会话、7种记忆条件），同时评估Direct QA准确率与用户满意度；提出MemUse基准，从部署中提取真实用户提示的记忆时刻，以整合感知的方式对自然对话响应进行评分，并对比Direct QA表现。

Result: 7种记忆条件下Direct QA准确率从19.7%到70.1%不等，但用户满意度无显著变化；同一系统在Direct QA上达78.8%，但在对话中仅自然引用7.9%的相关事实（71个百分点的差距）；在记忆时刻内，自然整合能力与满意度相关，而Direct QA则无关。

Conclusion: 现有Direct QA基准与用户满意度追踪的是不同的能力维度，需要以"自然整合"为导向的评估方法（如MemUse）来更准确地预测用户体验。

Abstract: Memory systems for conversational LLMs are conventionally evaluated by direct, fact-seeking questions about prior dialogue (Direct QA): can the model recall fact X from a prior conversation? We tested whether higher Direct QA accuracy correlates with higher user satisfaction in a 4-month deployment (40 users, 1,872 sessions, 7 memory conditions). Existing-benchmark Direct QA varies from 19.7% to 70.1% across the 7 conditions, but satisfaction does not change. We hypothesize that existing benchmarks and user satisfaction are tracking different capabilities: benchmarks measure elicited retrieval (recall when asked), while conversation requires natural integration (detecting relevance and naturally weaving prior context into a response). To examine this, we introduce MemUse, a set of real user-cued memory moments drawn from the deployment, scored by an integration-aware judgment of the natural conversational response. Holding the model and context fixed, the same system that scores 78.8% on Direct QA references only 7.9% of those facts in conversation -- a 71-point gap. Within these moments, Natural Integration is associated with satisfaction, whereas Direct QA is not. We release the deployment corpus and MemUse together with all judgments and scoring prompts at https://github.com/ryuichi-sumida/memuse.

</details>


### [122] ['Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24191)
*Fawzia Zehra,Kara-Isitt,Sonal Khosla,Stephen Swift*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 乌尔都语（全球第十大语言，2.46亿使用者）几乎完全缺席主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议。研究发现，在乌尔都语脚本中，五个主流LLM的原始脚本与英文翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性从15.9%到31.6%不等，'乌尔都语漏检'率（英文翻译中被标记为有害但原始脚本中通过正常的比例）中位数为4.3%。小规模开源模型的不稳定性和漏检率显著高于前沿闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌尔都语作为世界第十大语言拥有2.46亿使用者，但几乎完全缺席主流LLM安全评估和WOAH会议。作者旨在调查这一缺席是否对内容审核可靠性产生可测量的实际后果。

Method: 测试五个大语言模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1），跨越六个数据集，涵盖Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码混合乌尔都语-英语。同时通过ACL Anthology API对九届ALW/WOAH会议的205篇论文进行完整枚举。

Result: 乌尔都语脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英文翻译分类的标签不稳定性为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）至31.6%（Qwen-2.5）；'Missed-in-Urdu'率（英文翻译中判定有害但原始脚本中放行的比例）为2.4%至9.9%，中位数4.3%。ACL Anthology API确认205篇论文中没有任何一篇专门针对乌尔都语的论文。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在乌尔都语的各种文字变体上提供了不平衡的安全保障，较小型开源权重模型相比前沿闭源模型表现出显著更高的不稳定性和漏检率。

Abstract: Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.

</details>


### [123] [Measuring Digital Labour Market Transitions with a Digital Semantic Score: An AI-Based Methodology Applied to the Dutch Labour Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24222)
*Sadegh Shahmohammadi,Xavier Pinho,Mairi Bowdler,Suhendan Adiguzel-van Zoelen,Joost van Genabeek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于AI的方法论，利用百万级荷兰职位档案数据，通过嵌入相似性搜索与大语言模型分类相结合的方式，将非结构化职位信息映射至标准化的ESCO职业体系，并引入"数字语义评分"（Digital Semantic Score）来衡量职位名称和技能与数字概念的关联强度，从而为监测荷兰劳动力市场的数字化变迁提供可扩展的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荷兰劳动力市场的数字化转型正在重塑职业语言、职业路径和岗位技能，但现有政策制定和劳动力市场分析缺乏细粒度（granular）的市场情报。传统的基于关键词的方法难以捕捉职业语言中数字概念的深层语义关联，需要更先进的工具来理解数字化在职业间的分布特征、职业转换路径和技能数字化的多维本质。

Method: 方法论包含三个核心组件：（1）基于嵌入的相似性搜索与大语言模型分类相结合，将非结构化职位描述映射至ESCO标准化职业；（2）引入数字语义评分（Digital Semantic Score），利用嵌入向量和余弦相似度将职位标题与技能分别与透明的数字锚点组和非数字参考组进行对比，超越关键词匹配以捕捉更广泛的数字语义；（3）在此基础上进行职业分布分析、职业转换路径分析和技能数字化分析。

Result: 研究发现数字化在劳动力市场中分布不均衡：数字职位语言在管理、专业和ICT相关职业中最突出，但正日益渗透到混合型商业、营销和自动化相关岗位。职业转换分析表明向数字化工作的流动具有路径依赖性；技能分析揭示了数字能力的多维本质，涵盖技术性、混合型和业务系统类技能。

Conclusion: 该研究通过结合档案数据、AI辅助职业分类和语义评分，推进了AI驱动的劳动力市场分析，提供了一个可扩展的监测框架。该方法论有助于识别新兴技能需求、支持再技能培训策略，并为解决荷兰技能错配和劳动力短缺问题提供政策参考。

Abstract: The digital transformation of the Dutch labour market is reshaping occupational language, career pathways, and job-related skills. Addressing these changes requires granular labour market intelligence. This paper develops an AI-based methodology to analyse digitalisation using data covering millions of Dutch job profiles. The methodology combines embedding-based similarity search and large language model classification to map unstructured job information to harmonised ESCO occupations. We also introduce a Digital Semantic Score that measures how strongly job titles and skills are associated with digital concepts relative to a non-digital reference. Using embeddings and cosine similarity to transparent digital and non-digital anchor groups, this indicator moves beyond keyword-based approaches by capturing broader digital meanings in occupational language and worker skill profiles. It enables analysis across occupations, career transitions, emerging job-title vocabulary, and skill digitality. The findings reveal that digitalisation is unevenly distributed across the labour market. Digital job-title language is most prominent among managerial, professional and ICT-related occupations, but is increasingly visible in hybrid business, marketing and automation-related roles. Career-transition analyses show that movement toward digital work is pathway-dependent, while skill analyses highlight the multidimensional nature of digital capability, encompassing technical, hybrid and business-systems skills. By combining profile data, AI-supported occupational classification and semantic scoring, this study advances AI-driven labour market analytics and provides a scalable framework for monitoring digital labour market change. The methodology helps identify emerging skill needs, support reskilling strategies, and inform policies addressing skills mismatches and labour shortages in the Netherlands.

</details>


### [124] [RecurSE: Bounded Recursive Self-Evaluation for LLM Rubric Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24231)
*Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Rongxiang Weng,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RecurSE（递归自评估）框架，通过模型自身的评估能力生成学习信号，在无需外部金标标注的情况下实现LLM-as-judge的有界递归自改进，并利用PAV（成对优势效度）作为无偏验证监控器来确定最优早停时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为裁判在评估开放式文本和引导后训练中至关重要，但改进裁判本身通常依赖昂贵的标注、奖励模型或从更强教师的蒸馏。现有方法缺乏无需外部金标监督的自改进路径，本文旨在探索何时可以发生自改进以及何时必须停止——这是有界递归自改进的核心问题。

Method: RecurSE采用两阶段闭环设计：Pass 1由可训练裁判在逐规则评分标准下评估候选回复；Pass 2由与策略同步的检查器副本依据元评分标准审计裁判推理，输出标量过程奖励。接口解耦在结构上隔离检查器的标量分数与裁判的判定词元，消除退化的词元复制捷径，防止自我奖励膨胀。同时引入PAV作为无偏验证监控器，联合追踪裁判准确性和检查器保真度，可靠识别最优早停窗口。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-E4B-it和Qwen3.6-27B三个模型上，RecurSE在医疗、成对比较、摘要和专业基准测试中取得一致的泛化提升。消融实验表明，同步的裁判-检查器协同进化优于冻结检查器、外部元裁判、自一致性以及规模更大教师蒸馏。此外，由该裁判筛选的偏好对能有效增强下游策略对齐。

Conclusion: 当自我产生的奖励有效性被显式解耦和监控时，LLM-as-judge的有界递归自改进是可行的。RecurSE证明了无需外部金标监督即可实现裁判模型的持续优化，并为递归自改进提供了理论边界和实践验证方案。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge is essential for evaluating open-ended text and steering post-training, yet improving the judge itself typically relies on expensive annotations, reward models, or distillation from stronger teachers. In this work, we eliminate external gold supervision from the RL training reward: the model's own evaluative capability generates learning signals for its optimization -- a closed-loop setting of bounded recursive self-improvement (RSI) termed Recursive Self-Evaluation (RecurSE). We study two central questions: when can self-improvement occur, and when must it stop? First, RecurSE pairs a trainable judge evaluating candidate responses under per-rule rubrics (Pass 1) with a synchronized policy-copy checker that audits the judge's reasoning against meta-rubrics to supply a scalar process reward (Pass 2). To enable learning, interface decoupling structurally isolates the checker's scalar score from the judge's verdict tokens, eliminating a degenerative token-copying shortcut that inflates self-assigned rewards. Second, because unanchored recursive learning is inherently bounded, Pairwise Advantage Validity (PAV) serves as an unbiased validation monitor that jointly tracks judge accuracy and checker fidelity to reliably identify the optimal early-stopping window. Across Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-E4B-it, and Qwen3.6-27B, RecurSE achieves consistent generalization gains across held-out medical, pairwise, summarization, and professional benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate that synchronized judge-checker co-evolution outperforms frozen checkers, external meta-judges, self-consistency, and scaled teacher distillation. Furthermore, preference pairs curated by our judge effectively enhance downstream policy alignment. Bounded RSI for LLM-as-judge is thus viable when self-produced reward validity is explicitly decoupled and monitored.

</details>


### [125] [ROBE: Reversed-Order-Biased-Experts for Extracting Extreme Long-tail Events from Historical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24268)
*Stella Verkijk,Piek Vossen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ROBE（Reversed-Order-Biased-Experts）方法，从17-18世纪荷兰历史语料库中提取50多种事件类型。该方法通过为低频事件创建专家分类器并采用反向偏置优先级策略，结合领域特定合成数据生成方法，相较于简单微调的编码器模型，在长尾类别上实现了F1值0.10的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 19世纪前的历史数据属于大语言模型预训练中未覆盖的小众领域，且目标事件在训练数据中注释极为稀少。作者旨在解决"长尾中的长尾"这一难题，即从极其稀少标注的事件中提取信息，挑战传统方法因频率偏差而忽视低频事件的问题。

Method: 提出了ROBE（Reversed-Order-Biased-Experts）方法：1）为训练数据中的事件子组创建专家分类器，分组依据为训练数据中的相似频率或语义相关性；2）对在代表性不足事件上训练的专家赋予更高预测优先级，以避免被频率偏差主导；3）提出一种受控的领域特定合成数据生成方法以增强训练数据。

Result: ROBE的两种实现在召回率上比简单微调的编码器模型提高了0.10，精确率提高了0.16。最佳模型在长尾类别组上实现了F1值0.10的提升。

Conclusion: ROBE方法通过反转专家分类器的优先级分配，有效地保护了长尾类别的识别性能，验证了在极其稀缺的历史领域数据中通过专家分类器组合和合成数据增强来提升事件提取性能的可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes methods to extract over 50 types of events from a Dutch historical corpus spanning the 17th and 18th centuries. The methods we propose aim to tackle the impossible: extracting the long-tail of the long-tail. Historic data from before the 19th century is in itself a niche domain not covered in the pre-training of Large Language Models, and we aim to extract events only very scarcely annotated in the training data available for this domain. We propose creating expert classifiers for subgroups of the events present in the training data. We make these groupings based on similar frequency in the training data or on semantic relatedness. Experts trained on underrepresented events are assigned higher priority when predicting to avoid being dominated by frequency biases. We refer to this new way of combining classifiers, specifically tailored to protect the long-tail, as ROBE: Reversed-Order-Biased-Experts. We also propose a controlled method to create domain-specific synthetic data. Our two implementations of ROBE outperform a simple fine-tuned encoder model with a .10 increase in recall and a .16 increase in precision respectively. The best model achieves a .10 increase in f1 for a group of long-tail classes in our niche data set.

</details>


### [126] [SENSESHIFT: Continuous Sentiment-Controlled Text Generation via Encoder-based Mask Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24304)
*Shahed Masoudian,Markus Frohmann,Emmanouil Karystinaios,Navid Rekabsaz,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SenseShift，一个基于编码器的细粒度句子级可控文本生成框架，利用双向注意力、量化情感信号和迭代掩码填充，在故事与评论生成任务上实现了比更大规模解码器基线更强的情感可控性，同时保持文本质量和跨域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感可控文本生成（CTG）方法主要基于解码器架构，存在两个关键局限：一是将情感简化为粗粒度类别标签（如积极/消极）或整篇文档的单一细粒度控制信号；二是忽略了句子级情感控制等更具挑战性的任务设置。这导致模型难以满足复杂的细粒度情感约束。

Method: SenseShift是一个基于编码器的框架，其核心设计包含三个要素：（1）双向注意力机制取代传统的因果注意力，使编码器能更好地建模局部上下文；（2）使用量化情感信号（quantized sentiment signals）表示目标情感强度，实现细粒度控制；（3）通过迭代掩码填充（iterative mask infilling）逐句生成文本，实现对指定句子的局部、精准的情感调控。

Result: 在故事生成和评论生成任务上的实证评估表明，SenseShift在情感可控性方面优于更大的解码器基线模型，同时在文本质量和跨域（out-of-domain）生成的鲁棒性上也表现更好。

Conclusion: SenseShift证明了编码器架构在细粒度句子级情感可控文本生成中的有效性，填补了长时间文本中句子级情感控制这一未充分探索的研究空白，为CTG领域提供了不同于主流解码器范式的新思路。

Abstract: Recent controllable text generation (CTG) for sentiment control has largely focused on decoder-based large language models, making causal attention the dominant paradigm. While effective for fluent generation, these models still struggle to satisfy complex constraints and follow fine-grained sentiment signals specified by users. Existing sentiment-aware CTG methods typically simplify the problem by treating sentiment either as a coarse categorical label (e.g., positive or negative) or as a single fine-grained control signal applied to an entire document. Consequently, more challenging settings such as sentence-level sentiment control within long-form text remain underexplored. To address these limitations, we introduce SenseShift , an encoder-based framework for fine-grained sentence-level CTG. Unlike standard decoder architectures, SenseShift leverages bidirectional attention, quantized sentiment signals, and iterative mask infilling to generate local sentences conditioned on target sentiment intensity. Empirical evaluations on story and review generation demonstrate that SenseShift achieves stronger sentiment controllability while maintaining text quality and robustness to out-of-domain generation compared to larger decoder-based baselines.

</details>


### [127] [Who is the Agent to Blame? Localizing Faithfulness and Citation Mistakes in Agentic Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24306)
*Eran Hirsch,David Wan,Han Wang,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal,Ido Dagan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对深度研究（DR）系统中引用召回率低的问题，提出了一种定位错误来源的评估方法，通过局部测试各个Agent的调用，结合四类错误分类法，识别出错误主要来源于编排器（orchestrator）而非单文档摘要Agent，并基于此提出两个简单干预措施，将引用召回率提升5%而不降低输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR系统生成的报告依赖引用作为事实忠实度的主要评估机制，但现有系统的引用召回率较差。由于DR系统是多Agent的复杂架构，信息在Agent间传递、内容和引用都可能在此过程中被"电话游戏"式地损坏，因此难以定位错误来源，也就难以针对性改进引用召回率。

Method: 提出一种评估方法，通过局部测试每个Agent的调用相对于其自身输入的事实忠实度（faithfulness）和可验证性（verifiability），精确定位每个错误是由哪个Agent引入的；同时提出四类错误分类法：幻觉（hallucination）、未引出输入依赖（uncited input reliance）、未引出输出（uncited output）、引用不足（insufficient citations）。将该方法应用于三个排名靠前的开源DR系统。

Result: 几乎所有Agent都会犯大量错误，唯一例外是摘要单个文档的Agent。错误的主导类型在不同Agent间存在系统性差异，编排器的错误主要是引用相关的。在AI-Q系统中，84.7%的最终报告错误源自编排器，其中约31%是幻觉，其余为引用相关错误。基于这些发现提出的两个简单干预措施将引用召回率提升了5%且未降低输出质量。

Conclusion: 通过局部化的错误溯源分析，可以有效诊断DR系统中各Agent的错误来源，且错误主要集中在编排器的引用环节。有针对性的轻量干预即可显著改善引用召回率，验证了该诊断方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Deep research (DR) systems produce long-form cited reports by orchestrating multiple agents that search and synthesize information from the web. Citations are the primary mechanism for evaluating the faithfulness of these reports, yet current DR systems exhibit poor citation recall. Moreover, improving citation recall is challenging because DR systems are complex multi-agent architectures where information passes through agents like a telephone game, and both content and citations can get corrupted along the way. We propose an evaluation method that pinpoints which agent introduced each error by locally testing agent invocations for faithfulness and verifiability relative to their own inputs. Furthermore, we propose a four-type taxonomy to categorize the discovered errors: hallucination, uncited input reliance, uncited output, or insufficient citations. Applying our method to three top-ranked open-source DR systems, we obtain actionable diagnostics. Almost every agent makes a lot of mistakes with the exception being those that summarize a single document. We find that the dominant error type varies systematically across agents, where the orchestrator mistakes are mostly citation-related. We find that 84.7% of final-report errors in AI-Q originate at the orchestrator, roughly 31% of them hallucinations and the rest citation mistakes. Guided by these insights, we demonstrate that two simple interventions raise citation recall by 5% without degrading output quality.

</details>


### [128] [Speech-to-SOAP: End-to-End Summarization of Medical Dialogues: KIT@BeTraC 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24327)
*Enes Yavuz Ugan,Fabian Retkowski,Yuka Ko,Thai-Binh Nguyen,Maike Züfle,Jan Niehues,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了KIT在BeTraC挑战赛轻量级赛道中的提交方案，核心贡献是一个可扩展的数据增强流水线，通过合成语音生成和自动生成的SOAP监督统一异构医疗对话数据集，实现语音基础模型的端到端语音到SOAP生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的指令跟随能力推动了摘要任务的发展，其中临床方案制定的抽取子任务尤为值得关注，因为它可以显著减少医护人员的等待时间和方案制定负担，使他们能够专注于核心的医疗工作。进一步地，直接从语音生成临床笔记（而非通过中间转录文本）可以减少处理时间并保留咳嗽等副语言线索，这些信息在基于转录的系统中可能会丢失。

Method: 提出了一个可扩展的数据增强流水线，该流水线通过合成语音生成统一异构医疗对话数据集，并利用自动生成的SOAP（主观-客观-评估-计划）监督信号，使语音基础模型能够针对端到端语音到SOAP生成任务进行稳健的适配。

Result: 该方案代表KIT在BeTraC挑战赛轻量级赛道中的正式提交，通过统一的数据增强流水线实现了语音基础模型的端到端speech-to-SOAP生成能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据增强流水线为端到端语音到临床笔记生成提供了一种可行路径，通过合成数据统一异构医疗对话并自动生成SOAP监督，有望减轻医护人员的方案制定负担。

Abstract: With the advent of Large Language Models and its instruction following capabilities a promising application is the task of summarization. Within this domain of task the extractive sub-task of clinical protocolling has emerged as a topic of particular interest as it can significantly reduce the downtime and protocolling burden of health-care workers thus enabling them to focus on their core work helping humans. A further step towards automation is the direct generation of clinical notes from speech without intermediate transcripts, reducing processing time while preserving information such as coughing or other paralinguistic cues that may be lost in transcript-based systems. To this end, we present KIT's submission to this years BeTraC challenge in the lightweight track. Our main contribution is a scalable data augmentation pipeline that unifies heterogeneous medical dialogue datasets through synthetic speech generation and automatically generated SOAP supervision, enabling robust adaptation of a speech foundation model for end-to-end speech-to-SOAP generation.

</details>


### [129] [SteerCheck: Attribution Specificity and Alignment Leakage in Activation-Steering Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24335)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SteerCheck，一种预注册的归因审计框架，用于系统评估激活引导（activation steering）对模型行为的影响是否真正特异于目标概念。通过对Qwen3-14B的960次干预精确回放，发现常见对照方法存在互补性局限：各向同性方向占据狭窄的近正交区域，而符号随机化同构方向常保留大量目标对齐。效果与符号随机化族内的符号余弦强相关（ρ=.94），25.3%的采样余弦超过0.5，所有超过观测均值的采样余弦均高于0.80。主要Qwen完整门为阴性（保护尾部在所有族中失败），跨数据集的迁移结果因模型而异，常规检测器校准不良，整体结论呈现条件性和混合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导（activation steering）技术可以改变模型行为，但现有研究未能证明这种效果是否特异于预期概念。缺乏系统的归因审计方法，使得研究者难以区分真实的概念特异性效应与无关的副作用，因此需要一套严格的审计框架来检验干预的特异性。

Method: 引入SteerCheck预注册归因审计框架，匹配非目标KL（off-target KL）并分离均值、保护尾部、极性、迁移和语义五类声明。对Qwen3-14B进行960次干预的精确回放，使用条件随机化检验（conditional randomization test），比较各向同性方向与符号随机化同构方向两类对照，并引入构造诊断量A和经验余弦分布来评估对齐泄漏（alignment leakage）。同时采用三方冻结评估和自动评判器进行开放生成评估。

Result: 各向同性方向与符号随机化方向存在互补性局限；效果与符号余弦强相关（ρ=.94），25.3%的随机采样余弦超过0.5，所有超过观测均值效应的采样余弦超过0.80。Qwen主要完整门为阴性，保护尾部在所有对照族中失败。连续边际转移仅在Qwen中发生，准确率转移在所有选定细胞中均不发生。语言控制通过Qwen和DeepSeek完整门，但DeepSeek解毒对照排除了分类分离，所有名义通过均对Γ=1.10敏感。自动评判器校准不良（macro-F1=.562），事实纠正仅在DeepSeek中受支持。

Conclusion: 对齐泄漏本身并不使条件随机化检验失效，但它限制了对照方法能够区分的范围，因此需要报告可交换性假设、构造诊断量A和经验余弦分布。SteerCheck使这些条件性和混合性结论变得可审计，为激活引导研究提供了更严谨的归因验证框架。

Abstract: Activation steering can change behaviour without establishing that the effect is specific to the intended concept. We introduce SteerCheck, a preregistered attribution audit that matches off-target KL and separates mean, protected-tail, polarity, transfer, and semantic claims. Exact replay of 960 Qwen3-14B interventions reveals complementary limits of common controls: isotropic directions occupy a narrow near-orthogonal region, whereas sign-randomized same-construction directions often retain substantial target alignment. Effect is strongly associated with signed cosine within the sign-randomized family ($ρ=.94$); $25.3\%$ of its draws exceed cosine $.5$, and every draw exceeding the observed mean effect has cosine above $.80$. This alignment leakage does not by itself invalidate a conditional randomization test; it limits what the comparator can distinguish and motivates reporting exchangeability assumptions, a construction diagnostic $A$, and the empirical cosine distribution. The primary Qwen complete gate remains negative because the protected tail fails all families. On independent data, continuous margin transfers only in Qwen and accuracy transfers in no selected cell. Prospectively registered language controls pass the complete gate in Qwen and DeepSeek, while a passing DeepSeek detox comparator rules out categorical separation; all nominal passes are sensitive to $Γ=1.10$. Frozen three-rater open-generation evaluation supports factual correction in DeepSeek but not Qwen; the automatic judge fails calibration (macro-F1 $.562$), so null-wide semantic results remain descriptive. SteerCheck makes these conditional and mixed conclusions auditable.

</details>


### [130] [FARCA: Fact-Aligned Reliability-Aware Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning with Factual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24350)
*Qiming Xie,Wenjie Zheng,Xiangqing Shen,Rui Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FARCA框架，通过细粒度事实验证与策略更新对齐，结合反事实证据归因计算可靠性权重，解决强化学习中嘈杂事实信用分配问题，提升模型事实性并保持通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于可验证奖励的强化学习训练中，结果驱动的奖励会导致大型语言模型产生幻觉风险。现有缓解方法引入过程级事实监督，但事实信号的粗粒度聚合以及缺乏对这些信号可靠性的评估，造成了事实验证与策略更新之间的不匹配，即"嘈杂事实信用分配"问题，具体表现为信用定位模糊和信用可靠性模糊两个方面。

Method: 提出FARCA（事实对齐的可靠性感知信用分配）策略优化框架。核心方法包括：(1) 将事实监督转化为局部化、可靠性加权的token级训练信号，通过对齐事实验证的粒度与策略更新的粒度实现细粒度信用定位；(2) 引入反事实证据归因，利用事实判断对关键证据的依赖程度作为验证可靠性的经验代理，计算可靠性权重；(3) 这些权重用于调节事实奖励和局部策略优势，降低不可靠信号对策略优化的影响。

Result: 在不同模型和多个事实推理基准上的实验表明，FARCA显著提升模型的事实性，同时保持通用推理能力。

Conclusion: FARCA通过细粒度信用定位和可靠性加权，有效缓解了强化学习训练中嘈杂事实信用分配问题，在提升事实性的同时不牺牲通用推理性能。

Abstract: To reduce the hallucination risk caused by outcome-driven rewards in large language models trained through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, existing mitigation approaches introduce process-level factual supervision. However, due to coarse-grained aggregation of factual signals and the lack of reliability assessment for these signals, they create a mismatch between fact verification and policy updates. We term this noisy factual credit assignment and decompose it into two aspects: credit localization ambiguity and credit reliability ambiguity. To address these issues, we propose FARCA (Fact-Aligned Reliability-Aware Credit Assignment), a policy optimization framework that transforms factual supervision into localized, reliability-weighted token-level training signals. FARCA achieves fine-grained credit localization by aligning the granularity of fact verification with that of policy updates. It further introduces counterfactual evidence attribution, which uses the dependence of a factual judgment on key evidence as an empirical proxy for verification reliability to compute reliability weights. These weights modulate factual rewards and local policy advantages, reducing the influence of potentially unreliable signals on policy optimization. Experiments across different models and multiple factual reasoning benchmarks show that FARCA significantly improves model factuality while preserving general reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [131] [Words, Spaces and Generative AI: Layers of language in contemporary architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24360)
*Anca-Simona Horvath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本章探讨语言作为建筑设计材料在生成式AI语境下的作用，提出建筑设计中三种语言形式（话语、编程语言、注释）的交织，并勾画出连接生成隐喻与生成式AI的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着文本到X生成式AI模型成为建筑实践的常见工具，语言在建筑中的重要性比以往任何时候都更突出，需要从语言学视角重新审视语言如何参与建筑设计过程。

Method: 从维特根斯坦出发，梳理乔姆斯基、拉科夫等语言学发展，结合Schön提出的概念隐喻与生成隐喻理论，将语言学思想与当代建筑设计和生成式文本到X工具相连接，提出三种语言形式（话语、编程语言、注释）交织的框架，并据此提出研究议程。

Result: 提出一个三方面的研究议程：(1) 对建筑文本进行语料库语言学研究（定量的主题建模与定性的话语分析）；(2) 将传播理论与信息科学引入建筑研究；(3) 承认不同自然语言具有不同的可供性，生成隐喻和概念隐喻因语言而异。

Conclusion: 语言是理解并改进生成式AI辅助建筑设计的关键维度，未来研究应沿着语料库研究、传播理论融合和多语言可供性三个方向推进，以弥合生成隐喻与生成式AI之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: Language can be considered a design material in architecture, and in the context of text-to-X generative AI models becoming a common tool for architectural practice, looking more closely at language is more important now than in the past. After describing some of the important developments in linguistics starting from Wittgenstein, and including the work of Chomsky, Lakoff, conceptual and generative metaphors as proposed by Schön, this chapter connects them to contemporary architectural design and generative text-to-X tools. The chapter builds on the idea that three main forms of language intertwine in architectural design done using generative AI, namely (I) discourse (or natural language which can contain professional terminology specific to our field), (II) programming languages (which are artificial languages sitting at the basis of all computational systems), and (III) annotations (as language elements attached to pieces of data). It concludes by outlining a research agenda for connecting generative metaphors to generative AI: (a) conducting corpus linguistics studies on architectural texts (using quantitative tools such as topic modelling, and qualitative tools such as discourse analysis); (b) bringing communication theory and information studies closer to architectural research and (c) taking into account that different (natural) languages come with different affordances meaning generative and conceptual metaphors differ in relation to this.

</details>


### [132] [Shortcut Before Circuit: Document Statistics Time In-Context Conflict Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24460)
*Yijun Liao,Fanwei Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过26M参数transformer在合成语言上的训练（75次运行均达到>=0.999的准确率），证明当上下文对同一事实给出两个值且规律（recency与rarity）完全共外延（coextensive）时，模型可以同时满足所有"held-in"评估准则，但因果干预揭示出的机制归属是高度不稳定的：13/25个单元格在符号占比上差异超过0.3，最大差异达0.879。论文构造了一个精确框架，说明共外延规则使目标函数对其无偏，机制的出现时间可以复现，但具体机制归属不可识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然数据中，一个事实被赋予两个值时，模型会依赖某个线索（recency、repetition、position），但现实中这些线索很少冲突，因此无法从行为数据判断模型究竟采用哪种机制。作者希望明确：仅凭"held-in"评估（例如准确率）能否区分并归属模型的因果机制，并给出一个可精确处理的构造来界定何时机制归因在数学上可用、何时不可用。

Method: 作者训练26M参数的transformer，在合成语言上使recency与rarity完全共外延（同现），然后通过一个最小因果编辑（翻转其中一个线索、同时保持truth、token数量和答案位置不变）将二者分离。他们检查per-cell readout在干预前后是否复现，跨三个种子统计符号占比差异；同时探查训练过程中模型从positional shortcut逃逸的时间（其天花板有闭式解，且随冗余单调变化），在逃逸前对属性归因做probe实验（32/75因果归因符号反转但准确率不变）。

Result: 所有75次训练都达到accuracy>=0.999（即使平凡启发式失效的情况也如此），因此无法用held-in评估区分模型。但干预下per-cell readout不复现：25个cell中13个在符号占比上跨三个种子差异超过0.3，最大差异0.879而标准误差仅0.025。该方差大小按优化释放程度排序。可复现的部分是时间：模型从positional shortcut逃逸的延迟（有闭式上限，随冗余单调）；在此逃逸之前probe，归因符号在32/75次运行中反转且准确率不变，说明以circuit formation为门控条件必要但不充分。这确立了"语料决定机制何时出现，而非何种机制出现"的判据。

Conclusion: 当一个语料中多条规律完全共外延，目标函数对选择哪一条无偏，此时held-in表现无法区分机制归属，而干预下机制读取不稳定——这与构造的理论预测一致。机制的出现时刻（timing）可复现并受语料冗余影响，但具体是哪一种机制在整个机制空间中不被数据识别。论文提供了一个精确的判据：当且仅当语料能打破线索间的共外延性时，机制归因才可用；否则属于"不可用情形"，本文构造使该情形精确化。

Abstract: When a context asserts two values for one fact, a model commits to a cue -- recency, repetition, position -- but natural data rarely makes these disagree, so behavior cannot reveal which. We train 26M-parameter transformers on a synthetic language where recency and rarity are exactly coextensive, and separate them with a minimal causal edit that inverts one cue while holding the truth, token count and answer position fixed. All 75 runs reach accuracy >= 0.999, including where the trivial heuristic fails, so no held-in evaluation distinguishes them. Under intervention the per-cell readout does not replicate: 13 of 25 cells differ by more than 0.3 in sign fraction across three seeds, the largest by 0.879 against a standard error of 0.025. The construction predicts this -- coextensive rules leave the objective indifferent between them -- and the variance is ordered by how much of the optimization each comparison releases. What replicates is timing: escape from a positional shortcut with a closed-form ceiling, monotone in redundancy. Probed before that escape, attribution reverses sign in 32 of 75 runs at unchanged accuracy, and gating on circuit formation is necessary but not sufficient. The corpus fixes when a mechanism appears, not which one -- a criterion for when mechanistic attribution to data is available at all, and our construction makes the unavailable case exact.

</details>


### [133] [Dataset Scarcity Limits Robust Evaluation of Multilingual Embedding Models: A Case Study of Slavic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24477)
*Ana Gjorgjevikj,Barbara Koroušić Seljak,Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对数据集稀缺条件下多语言嵌入基准评估的二维分析框架，应用于MTEB基准的斯拉夫语子集，从任务特定和跨任务两个层面分析排名稳健性、模型一致性和证据强度，发现严重的基准稀疏性，并识别出一组高迁移性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言文本嵌入模型虽广泛应用于跨语言迁移任务，但其评估在资源高低不同的语言间严重不均衡。现有评估方法无法在数据集稀缺时可靠地判断模型排名和结论的稳健性，亟需一个专门针对这一场景的两维分析框架。

Method: 提出两维分析框架，将任务特定评估与跨任务评估相结合，联合分析三个互补方面：(1) 排名稳健性——评估排名方法变化和基准数据集构成变化下模型排名的稳定性；(2) 模型一致性——评估模型在不同任务间的泛化能力；(3) 证据强度——引入Evidence Strength Score量化基准结论的可靠性，综合考虑数据集可用性、多样性和稳健性可评估性。框架应用于MTEB基准的斯拉夫语子集。

Result: 分析揭示了严重的基准稀疏性：许多斯拉夫语言-任务对仅依赖单一数据集或高度相关的基准集合，限制了稳健结论的推导能力。跨任务分析识别出一小群高迁移性模型，包括llama-embed-nemotron-8b、multilingual-e5-large-instruct和Qwen3-Embedding系列，在斯拉夫语言和任务上表现一致优异。

Conclusion: 基准排名和稳健性结论必须与证据强度标识联合解读；基准稀缺性是多语言可信评估的主要障碍，研究结果凸显了构建更丰富、更多样化的多语言评估基准的必要性。
